# The Immigration (European Economic Area) Regulations 2016

> Text as at 2026-08-12.

2016 No. 1052

Immigration

The Immigration (European Economic Area) Regulations 2016 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

Made *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

2nd November 2016 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

Laid before Parliament *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

3rd November 2016 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

Coming into force in accordance with regulation 1(2) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

The Secretary of State, being a Minister designated *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00001] for the purposes of section 2(2) of the European Communities Act 1972 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00002] in relation to measures relating to rights of entry into, and residence in, the United Kingdom, in exercise of the powers conferred by that section and those conferred by section 109 of the Nationality, Immigration and Asylum Act 2002 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00003], makes the following Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^c-e0f964ca09eed97254db0ea199f70d21][^c-5849a5de040a2a9504be983d5d340972]

## PART 1 (affected by repeal, see note) — PRELIMINARY (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 1. Citation and commencement (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) These Regulations may be cited as the Immigration (European Economic Area) Regulations 2016. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) These Regulations come into force— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) for the purposes of this regulation, regulation 44 and Schedule 5 (transitory provisions), on 25th November 2016; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) for all other purposes, on 1st February 2017. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 2. General interpretation (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-5e03f34e71c8e9e977369fca2fcc1bdf][^c-94be05bb3e66fad301af7051d783c414][^c-12642e52161187b6f9b1219c1a9e9ce3][^c-0d72860132a78bced27d686a25d893a7][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) In these Regulations— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- “the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]” means the Immigration Act 1971 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00004];
- “the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1999 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]” means the Immigration and Asylum Act 1999 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00005];
- “the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2002 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]” means the Nationality, Immigration and Asylum Act 2002; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- “the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2006 Regulations *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]” means the Immigration (European Economic Area) Regulations 2006 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00006];
- “the[^c-db6e2ecabb4832594fbd7a41de180f88] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2016 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]” means the Immigration Act 2016; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- “civil partner” does not include— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (a) a party to a civil partnership of convenience; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (b) the civil partner (“C”) of a person (“P”) where a spouse, civil partner or durable partner of C or P is already present in the United Kingdom; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- “civil partnership of convenience” includes a civil partnership entered into for the purpose of using these Regulations, or any other right conferred by the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EU *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] Treaties, as a means to circumvent— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (a) immigration rules applying to non-EEA nationals (such as any applicable requirement under the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] to have leave to enter or remain in the United Kingdom); or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (b) any other criteria that the party to the civil partnership of convenience would otherwise have to meet in order to enjoy a right to reside under these Regulations or the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EU *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] Treaties; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- “Common Travel Area” has the meaning given in section 1(3) of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137];
- “decision maker” means the Secretary of State, an immigration officer or an entry clearance officer (as the case may be); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- “deportation order” means an order made under regulation 32(3); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- “derivative residence card” means a card issued to a person under regulation 20; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- “derivative right to reside” means a right to reside under regulation 16; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- “document certifying permanent residence” means a document issued under regulation 19(1); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- “durable partner” does not include— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (a) a party to a durable partnership of convenience; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (b) the durable partner (“D”) of a person (“P”) where a spouse, civil partner or durable partner of D or P is already present in the United Kingdom and where that marriage, civil partnership or durable partnership is subsisting; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- “durable partnership of convenience” includes a durable partnership entered into for the purpose of using these Regulations, or any other right conferred by the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EU *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] Treaties, as a means to circumvent— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (a) immigration rules applying to non-EEA nationals (such as any applicable requirement under the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] to have leave to enter or remain in the United Kingdom); or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (b) any other criteria that the party to the durable partnership of convenience would otherwise have to meet in order to enjoy a right to reside under these Regulations or the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EU *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] Treaties; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- “EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] decision” means a decision under these Regulations that concerns— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (a) a person’s entitlement to be admitted to the United Kingdom; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (b) a person’s entitlement to be issued with or have renewed, or not to have revoked, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] an EEA family permit,[^c-e243709a7b1b8f07932cd2d7eed42a6a] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] a registration certificate, residence card, derivative residence card, document certifying permanent residence or permanent residence card (but does not include *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] a decision to reject an application for the above documentation as invalid[^c-03cbba097217f6cd0cf5b3a39dc02d19] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]);
  - (c) a person’s removal from the United Kingdom; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (d) the cancellation, under regulation 25, of a person’s right to reside in the United Kingdom, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

  but does not include *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] [^c-b0507730f771c9e04ae58a91094a5975]... a decision to *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] refuse[^c-823d1609d7fc36896a6438c1cdadccf5] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] an application under regulation 26(4) (misuse of a right to reside: material change of circumstances), or any decisions under regulation 33 (human rights considerations and interim orders to suspend removal) or 41 (temporary admission to submit case in person); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- “EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] family permit” means a document issued under regulation 12; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- “[^c-4785dba0f3af8859b72f6c8a64190f59] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national” means— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (a) a national of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State who is not also a British citizen; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (b) a national of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State who is also a British citizen and who prior to acquiring British citizenship exercised a right to reside as such a national, in accordance with regulation 14 or 15, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

  save that a person does not fall within paragraph (b) if the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State of which they are a national became a member State after that person acquired British citizenship. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- “EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State” means— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (a) a member State, other than the United Kingdom; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (b) Liechtenstein, Iceland, Norway or Switzerland; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- “entry clearance” has the meaning given in section 33(1) of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00007];
- “entry clearance officer” means a person responsible for the grant or refusal of entry clearance; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- “exclusion order” means an order made under regulation 23(5); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- “indefinite leave”, “immigration laws” and “immigration rules” have the meanings given in section 33(1) of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137];
- “marriage of convenience” includes a marriage entered into for the purpose of using these Regulations, or any other right conferred by the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EU *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] Treaties, as a means to circumvent— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (a) immigration rules applying to non-EEA nationals (such as any applicable requirement under the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] to have leave to enter or remain in the United Kingdom); or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (b) any other criteria that the party to the marriage of convenience would otherwise have to meet in order to enjoy a right to reside under these Regulations or the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EU *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] Treaties; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- “military service” means service in the armed forces of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- “permanent residence card” means a document issued under regulation 19(2); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- “qualifying *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State residence card” means a valid document called a “Residence card of a family member of a Union Citizen” issued under Article 10 of Council *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] [Directive 2004/38/EC *(affected by repeal, see note)*](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/eudr/2004/0038)[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00008] (as applied, where relevant, by the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] agreement) by any *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State (except Switzerland) to a non-EEA family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national as proof of the holder’s right of residence in that State; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- “registration certificate” means a certificate issued under regulation 17; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- “relevant *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national” in relation to an extended family member has the meaning given in regulation 8(6); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- “residence card” means a card issued under regulation 18; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- “right to reside” means a right to reside in the United Kingdom under these Regulations (or where so specified, a right to reside under a particular regulation); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- “spouse” does not include— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (a) a party to a marriage of convenience; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (b) the spouse (“S”) of a person (“P”) where a spouse, civil partner or durable partner of S or P is already present in the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) Section 11 of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] (construction of references to entry) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00009] applies for the purpose of determining whether a person has entered the United Kingdom for the purpose of these Regulations as it applies for the purpose of determining whether a person has entered the United Kingdom for the purpose of that Act. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 3. Continuity of residence (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-9b763dd78acbd2013ab4f40849e94c27][^c-4d4bef7135fe71091da4c70c2cf1b8c0][^c-c214aa82610c749a2f7e2dfd74ff6a37][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) This regulation applies for the purpose of calculating periods of continuous residence in the United Kingdom under these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) Continuity of residence is not affected by— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) periods of absence from the United Kingdom which do not exceed six months in total in any year; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) periods of absence from the United Kingdom on compulsory military service; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) one absence from the United Kingdom not exceeding twelve months for an important reason such as pregnancy and childbirth, serious illness, study or vocational training or an overseas posting. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) Continuity of residence is broken when— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) a person serves a sentence of imprisonment; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) a deportation or exclusion order is made in relation to a person; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) a person is removed from the United Kingdom under these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) Paragraph (3)(a) applies, in principle, to an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national who has resided in the United Kingdom for at least ten years, but it does not apply where the Secretary of State considers that— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) prior to serving a sentence of imprisonment, the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national had forged integrating links with the United Kingdom; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) the effect of the sentence of imprisonment was not such as to break those integrating links; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) taking into account an overall assessment of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national’s situation, it would not be appropriate to apply paragraph (3)(a) to the assessment of that *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national’s continuity of residence. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 4. “Worker”, “self-employed person”, “self-sufficient person” and “student” (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-e5ca1accf00988d68cfc298eea554cd9][^c-c92922e00d4d2b1031ffd3cd5ebac842][^c-9393e7ec4f37b4425f13f8de0fad6361][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) In these Regulations— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) “worker” means a worker within the meaning of Article 45 of the Treaty on the Functioning of the European Union *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00010];
- (b) “self-employed person” means a person who is established in the United Kingdom in order to pursue activity as a self-employed person in accordance with Article 49 of the Treaty on the Functioning of the European Union *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00011];
- (c) “self-sufficient person” means a person who has— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) sufficient resources not to become a burden on the social assistance system of the United Kingdom during the person’s period of residence; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] comprehensive sickness insurance cover in the United Kingdom; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (d) “student” means a person who— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) is enrolled, for the principal purpose of following a course of study (including vocational training), at a public or private establishment which is— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
    - (aa (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] financed from public funds; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
    - (bb (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] otherwise recognised by the Secretary of State as an establishment which has been accredited for the purpose of providing such courses or training within the law or administrative practice of the part of the United Kingdom in which the establishment is located; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] has comprehensive sickness insurance cover in the United Kingdom; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (iii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] has assured the Secretary of State, by means of a declaration, or by such equivalent means as the person may choose, that the person has sufficient resources not to become a burden on the social assistance system of the United Kingdom during the person’s intended period of residence. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) For the purposes of paragraphs (3) and (4) below, “relevant family member” means a family member of a self-sufficient person or student who is residing in the United Kingdom and whose right to reside is dependent upon being the family member of that student or self-sufficient person. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) In sub-paragraphs (1)(c) and (d)— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) the requirement for the self-sufficient person or student to have sufficient resources not to become a burden on the social assistance system of the United Kingdom during the intended period of residence is only satisfied if the resources available to the student or self-sufficient person and any of their relevant family members are sufficient to avoid the self-sufficient person or student and all their relevant family members from becoming such a burden; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) the requirement for the student or self-sufficient person to have comprehensive sickness insurance cover in the United Kingdom is only satisfied if such cover extends to cover both the student or self-sufficient person and all their relevant family members. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) In paragraph (1)(c) and (d) and paragraph (3), the resources of the student or self-sufficient person and, where applicable, any of their relevant family members, are to be regarded as sufficient if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) they exceed the maximum level of resources which a British citizen (including the resources of the British citizen’s family members) may possess if the British citizen is to become eligible for social assistance under the United Kingdom benefit system; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) paragraph (a) does not apply but, taking into account the personal circumstances of the person concerned and, where applicable, all their relevant family members, it appears to the decision maker that the resources of the person or persons concerned should be regarded as sufficient. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(5) For the purposes of regulation 16(2) (criteria for having a derivative right to reside), references in this regulation to “family members” includes a “primary carer” as defined in regulation 16(8). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 5. “Worker or self-employed person who has ceased activity” (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-36366b394dca52b81b096606a3e74e3a][^c-01e597b2dfd8ee69f400bc98ccd3c4f4][^c-ecf037a0128e15f181845f1a17bfb9c1][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) In these Regulations, “worker or self-employed person who has ceased activity” means an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national who satisfies a condition in paragraph (2), (3), (4) or (5). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) The condition in this paragraph is that the person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) terminates activity as a worker or self-employed person and— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) had reached the age of entitlement to a state pension on terminating that activity; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] in the case of a worker, ceases working to take early retirement; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) pursued activity as a worker or self-employed person in the United Kingdom for at least 12 months prior to the termination; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) resided in the United Kingdom continuously for more than three years prior to the termination. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) The condition in this paragraph is that the person terminates activity in the United Kingdom as a worker or self-employed person as a result of permanent incapacity to work; and— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) had resided in the United Kingdom continuously for more than two years *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] immediately[^c-0dde04d3dcf47c8a038e3c5fa2a28e19] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] prior to the termination; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) the incapacity is the result of an accident at work or an occupational disease that entitles the person to a pension payable in full or in part by an institution in the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) The condition in this paragraph is that the person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) is active as a worker or self-employed person in an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State but retains a place of residence in the United Kingdom and returns, as a rule, to that place at least once a week; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) immediately[^c-be456ec7b379aff08fef229dde3e5a6d] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] prior to becoming so active in the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State, had been continuously resident and continuously active as a worker or self-employed person in the United Kingdom for at least three years. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(5) A person who satisfied the condition in paragraph (4)(a) but not the condition in paragraph (4)(b) must, for the purposes of paragraphs (2) and (3), be treated as being active and resident in the United Kingdom during any period during which that person is working or self-employed in the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(6) The conditions in paragraphs (2) and (3) as to length of residence and activity as a worker or self-employed person do not apply in relation to a person whose spouse or civil partner is a British citizen. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(7) Subject to regulation 6(2), periods of— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) inactivity for reasons not of the person’s own making; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) inactivity due to illness or accident; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) in the case of a worker, involuntary unemployment duly recorded by the relevant employment office, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

must be treated as periods of activity as a worker or self-employed person, as the case may be. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 6. “Qualified person” (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-596e2ae8a410fd6f1dd36d1bbf107fe3][^c-c89d09e826945e91ba6220a281b14688][^c-f6ae09b77d46930dc7918046442d9342][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) In these Regulations— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- “jobseeker” means an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national who satisfies conditions A, B and, where relevant, C; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- “qualified person” means a person who is an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national and in the United Kingdom as— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (a) a jobseeker; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (b) a worker; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (c) a self-employed person; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (d) a self-sufficient person; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (e) a student; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- “relevant period” means— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (a) in the case of a person retaining worker status under paragraph (2)(b) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] or self-employed person status under paragraph (4)(b)[^c-606250d7bc2bed2ff13ccd244249deec] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137], a continuous period of six months; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (b) in the case of a jobseeker, 91 days, minus the cumulative total of any days during which the person concerned previously enjoyed a right to reside as a jobseeker, not including any days prior to a continuous absence from the United Kingdom of at least 12 months. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) A person who is no longer working must continue to be treated as a worker provided that the person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) is temporarily unable to work as the result of an illness or accident; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) is in duly recorded involuntary unemployment after having been employed in the United Kingdom for at least one year, provided the person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) has registered as a jobseeker with the relevant employment office; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] satisfies conditions A and B; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) is in duly recorded involuntary unemployment after having been employed in the United Kingdom for less than one year, provided the person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) has registered as a jobseeker with the relevant employment office; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] satisfies conditions A and B; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (d) is involuntarily unemployed and has embarked on vocational training; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (e) has voluntarily ceased working and has embarked on vocational training that is related to the person’s previous employment. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) A person to whom paragraph (2)(c) applies may only retain worker status for a maximum of six months. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4)[^c-5be01f35d7e096bebee3039f6e86425e] A person who is no longer in self-employment must continue to be treated as a self-employed person provided that the person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) is temporarily unable to engage in activities as a self-employed person as the result of an illness or accident; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) is in duly recorded involuntary unemployment after having worked as a self-employed person in the United Kingdom for at least one year provided the person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) has registered as a jobseeker with the relevant employment office; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] satisfies conditions D and E; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) is in duly recorded involuntary unemployment after having worked as a self-employed person in the United Kingdom for less than one year, provided the person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) has registered as a jobseeker with the relevant employment office; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] satisfies conditions D and E; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (d) is involuntarily no longer in self-employment and has embarked on vocational training; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (e) has voluntarily ceased self-employment and has embarked on vocational training that is related to the person’s previous occupation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4A) A person to whom paragraph (4)(c) applies may only retain self-employed person status for a maximum of six months. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4B) Condition D is that the person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) entered the United Kingdom as a self-employed person or in order to seek employment as a self-employed person; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) is present in the United Kingdom seeking employment or self-employment, immediately after enjoying a right to reside under sub-paragraphs (c) to (e) of the definition of qualified person in paragraph (1) (disregarding any period during which self-employed status was retained pursuant to paragraph (4)(b) or (c)). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4C) Condition E is that the person provides evidence of seeking employment or self-employment and having a genuine chance of being engaged. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(5) Condition A is that the person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) entered the United Kingdom in order to seek employment; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) is present in the United Kingdom seeking employment, immediately after enjoying a right to reside under *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] sub-paragraphs (b), (d) or (e)[^c-97d0661f8edae5cbcce47fe6944c1dff] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] of the definition of qualified person in paragraph (1) (disregarding any period during which worker status was retained pursuant to paragraph (2)(b) or (c)). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(6) Condition B is that the person provides evidence of seeking employment and having a genuine chance of being engaged. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(7) A person may not retain the status of— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) a worker under paragraph (2)(b); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] [^c-c834e5646120f4b76bbec6ec47568687]...
- (b) a jobseeker; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] or (c) a self-employed person under paragraph (4)(b)[^c-8bb7fb2a3e70dfae387da431b0b5f850] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

for longer than the relevant period without providing compelling evidence of continuing to seek employment and having a genuine chance of being engaged. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(8) Condition C applies where the person concerned has, previously, enjoyed a right to reside under this regulation as a result of satisfying conditions A and B *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] or, as the case may be, conditions D and E[^c-2021f7e0fbd3edfaa898d27780ae2f6d] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]—

- (a) in the case of a person to whom paragraph (2)(b) or (c) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] or (4)(b) or (c)[^c-4502328a96a55bbd538db45eba0b0790] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] applied, for at least six months; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) in the case of a jobseeker, for at least 91 days in total, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

unless the person concerned has, since enjoying the above right to reside, been continuously absent from the United Kingdom for at least 12 months. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(9) Condition C is that the person has had a period of absence from the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(10) Where condition C applies— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) paragraph (7) does not apply; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) condition B *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] or, as the case may be, condition E[^c-a0f8e591d02af36a6438145136bd2290] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] has effect as if “compelling” were inserted before “evidence”. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 7. “Family member” (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-25114bbd09e7db1eb9c7e4733314bdcf][^c-aac15664a0eb724e888e68c02260beef][^c-bf93aa130f56c9447c3560c79f66e316][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) In these Regulations, “family member” means, in relation to a person (“A”)— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) A’s spouse or civil partner; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) A’s direct descendants, or the direct descendants of A’s spouse or civil partner who are either— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) aged under 21; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] dependants of A, or of A’s spouse or civil partner; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) dependent direct relatives in A’s ascending line, or in that of A’s spouse or civil partner. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) Where A is a student residing in the United Kingdom otherwise than under regulation 13 (initial right of residence), a person is not a family member of A under paragraph (1)(b) or (c) unless— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) in the case of paragraph (1)(b), the person is the dependent child of A or of A’s spouse or civil partner; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) A also falls within one of the other categories of qualified person mentioned in regulation 6(1). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) A person (“B”) who is an extended family member and has been issued with an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] family permit, a registration certificate or a residence card must be treated as a family member of A, provided— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) B continues to satisfy the conditions in regulation *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 8(1A),[^c-00e27fd0969c3198e8bf389fb33ebdfc] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 8(2), (3), (4) or (5); and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] family permit, registration certificate or residence card remains in force. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) A must be an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national unless regulation 9 applies (family members *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] and extended family members[^c-b3f45c459fd1a13b5af71fd2d3fd4f02] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] of British citizens). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 8. “Extended family member” (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d94b96f5829e57b00160b3f6a0d0cd84][^c-b486998e20865b07ca58173f24fb6917][^c-7081fb5a1c60ea07fbec3c416048b08d][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) In these Regulations “extended family member” means a person who is not a family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national under regulation 7(1)(a), (b) or (c) and who satisfies a condition in paragraph *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] (1A),[^c-8dff15a230884f5708258039faef190e] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] (2), (3), (4) or (5). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1A)[^c-d91fcb2a911343f3e529c2e979704dc8] The condition in this paragraph is that the person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) is under the age of 18; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) is subject to a non-adoptive legal guardianship order in favour of an EEA national that is recognised under the national law of the state in which it was contracted; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) has lived with the EEA national since their placement under the guardianship order; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (d) has created family life with the EEA national; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (e) has a personal relationship with the EEA national that involves dependency on the EEA national and the assumption of parental responsibility, including legal and financial responsibilities, for that person by the EEA national. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) The condition in this paragraph is that the person is— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) a relative of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) residing in a country other than the United Kingdom and is dependent upon the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national or is a member of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national’s household; and either— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) is accompanying the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national to the United Kingdom or wants to join the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national in the United Kingdom; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] has joined the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national in the United Kingdom and continues to be dependent upon the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national, or to be a member of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national’s household. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) The condition in this paragraph is that the person is a relative of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national and on serious health grounds, strictly requires the personal care of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] or the spouse or civil partner of the EEA national[^c-74880a3fe3eff77bcb2873e84fcdc735] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137].

(4) The condition in this paragraph is that the person is a relative of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national and would meet the requirements in the immigration rules (other than those relating to entry clearance) for indefinite leave to enter or remain in the United Kingdom as a dependent relative of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(5) The condition in this paragraph is that the person is the partner (other than a civil partner) of, and in a durable relationship with, an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] or the child (under the age of 18) of that partner[^c-516d9d9671daf27031a847ece80f4371] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137], and is able to prove this to the decision maker. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(6) In these Regulations, “relevant *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national” means, in relation to an extended family member— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) referred to in paragraph (2), (3) or (4), the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national to whom the extended family member is related; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) referred to in paragraph (5), the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national who is the durable partner of the extended family member. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(7) In *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] paragraphs (2), (3) and (4)[^c-8b7bd410befdc1692caa27e274c749b0] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137], “relative of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national” includes a relative of the spouse or civil partner of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] [^c-240cb8c545d1e54ae5f1ef3e9d6ec9d0]....

(8)[^c-14106c7d667b9c5b254303b5d13f9408] Where an extensive examination of the personal circumstances of the applicant is required under these Regulations, it must include examination of the following— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) the best interests of the applicant, particularly where the applicant is a child; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) the character and conduct of the applicant; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) whether an EEA national would be deterred from exercising their free movement rights if the application was refused. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

Family members (affected by repeal, see note) and extended family members (affected by repeal, see note) of British citizens (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-9d40f38b7f2ac60c3a1ca3a649e1d056][^c-65e7e4b7afa77873aa9d39a5fa971f20][^c-2f6f464656af55818eec2ee06a7537e8][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^c-b799d2b1c73e3484b1c4b02dcd726767][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (c) F or EFM[^c-0678038b0bf36fbf8b472ea4f1da1c53] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] and BC’s residence in the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State was genuine. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (f)[^c-49aef953280ee71875939de07b5b44b4] the conditions in sub-paragraphs (a), (b) and (c) have been met concurrently. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) where the purpose of the residence in the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State was as a means for circumventing any immigration laws applying to non-EEA nationals to which F *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] or EFM[^c-e71056bd9665f7b5e064241b109a79d6] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] would otherwise be subject (such as any applicable requirement under the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] to have leave to enter or remain in the United Kingdom); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] [^c-b530903c7b6df63ba0b39508c0705333]...

- (c) in assessing whether BC can be treated as a jobseeker as defined in regulation 6(1), BC is not required to satisfy conditions A and, where it would otherwise be relevant, condition C. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 9A. Dual national: national of an (affected by repeal, see note) EEA (affected by repeal, see note) State who acquires British citizenship (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-36c9676ff25017c0bfd36a2be7d553e2][^c-047c931b40a00f312ac618ec9ae1f6e1][^c-9f155cdcf64d1f48131d862833a9ba60][^c-3925dfc890738d9bfd0d4a0eeef42731][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) In this regulation “DN” means a person within paragraph (b) of the definition of “ *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national” in regulation 2(1). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) DN who comes within the definition of “qualified person” in regulation 6(1) is only a qualified person for the purpose of these Regulations if DN— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) came within the definition of “qualified person” at the time of acquisition of British citizenship; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) has not at any time subsequent to the acquisition of British citizenship lost the status of qualified person. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) Regulation 15 only applies to DN, or to the family member of DN who is not an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national, if DN satisfies the condition in paragraph (4). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) The condition in this paragraph is that at the time of acquisition of British citizenship DN either— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) was a qualified person; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) had acquired a right of permanent residence in accordance with these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 10. “Family member who has retained the right of residence” (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-78d07bb5c097375f8589de62ef2d03bb][^c-5b521632abf2ba07a44303a9cf061b92][^c-03247fbd3222204489d2f0df13ac5d33][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) In these Regulations, “family member who has retained the right of residence” means, subject to paragraphs (8) and (9), a person who satisfies a condition in paragraph (2), (3), (4) or (5). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) The condition in this paragraph is that the person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) was a family member of a qualified person or of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national with a right of permanent residence when the qualified person or the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national with the right of permanent residence died; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) resided in the United Kingdom in accordance with these Regulations for at least the year immediately before the death of the qualified person or the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national with a right of permanent residence; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) satisfies the condition in paragraph (6). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) The condition in this paragraph is that the person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) is the direct descendant of— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) a qualified person or an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national with a right of permanent residence who has died; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] a person who ceased to be a qualified person on ceasing to reside in the United Kingdom; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (iii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] the spouse or civil partner of the qualified person or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national described in sub-paragraph (i) immediately preceding that qualified person or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national’s death; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (iv (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] the spouse or civil partner of the person described in sub-paragraph (ii); and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) was attending an educational course in the United Kingdom immediately before the qualified person or the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national with a right of permanent residence died, or ceased to be a qualified person, and continues to attend such a course. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) The condition in this paragraph is that the person is the parent with actual custody of a child who satisfies the condition in paragraph (3). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(5) The condition in this paragraph is that the person (“A”)— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) ceased to be a family member of a qualified person or an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national with a right of permanent residence on the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] initiation of proceedings for the termination[^c-73e07c1e7ee9d07f42d156aee30d0995] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] of the marriage or civil partnership of A; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) was residing in the United Kingdom in accordance with these Regulations at the date of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] initiation of proceedings for the termination *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137];
- (c) satisfies the condition in paragraph (6); and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (d) either— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) prior to the initiation of the proceedings for the termination of the marriage or the civil partnership, the marriage or civil partnership had lasted for at least three years and the parties to the marriage or civil partnership had resided in the United Kingdom for at least one year during its duration; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] the former spouse or civil partner of the qualified person or the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national with a right of permanent residence has custody of a child of that qualified person or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (iii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] the former spouse or civil partner of the qualified person or the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national with a right of permanent residence has the right of access to a child of that qualified person or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national, where the child is under the age of 18 and where a court has ordered that such access must take place in the United Kingdom; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (iv (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] the continued right of residence in the United Kingdom of A is warranted by particularly difficult circumstances, such as where A or another family member has been a victim of domestic violence whilst the marriage or civil partnership was subsisting. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(6) The condition in this paragraph is that the person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) is not an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national but would, if the person were an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national, be a worker, a self-employed person or a self-sufficient person under regulation 6; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) is the family member of a person who falls within paragraph (a). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(7) In this regulation, “educational course” means a course within the scope of Article 10 of Council Regulation ( *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]EU *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]) No. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 492/2011[^f00012].

(8) A person (“P”) does not satisfy a condition in paragraph (2), (3), (4) or (5) if, at the first time P would otherwise have satisfied the relevant condition, P had a right of permanent residence under regulation 15. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(9) A family member who has retained the right of residence ceases to enjoy that status on acquiring a right of permanent residence under regulation 15. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

## PART 2 (affected by repeal, see note) — EEA (affected by repeal, see note) RIGHTS (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 11. Right of admission to the United Kingdom (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-6faba95cfb24e9f8f72e38232ff3964c][^c-5903637439a1f8295765f32131ac6449][^c-9842370e9efc520c941f42b6013a3476][^c-e63378e761f73e335bfd60fa496fe499][^c-4d8d7f0a1c10ee04e9b23cf4a1ab7ee2][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) An *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national must be admitted to the United Kingdom on arrival if the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national produces a valid national identity card or passport issued by an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) A person who is not an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national must be admitted to the United Kingdom if that person is— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) a family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national and produces on arrival a valid passport and qualifying *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State residence card, provided the conditions in regulation 23(4) (family member of *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national must accompany or join *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national with right to reside) are met; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) a family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national, a family member who has retained the right of residence, a person who meets the criteria in paragraph (5) or a person with a right of permanent residence under regulation 15 and produces on arrival— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) a valid passport; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] a valid *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] family permit, residence card, derivative residence card or permanent residence card. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) An immigration officer must not place a stamp in the passport of a person admitted to the United Kingdom under this regulation who is not an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national if the person produces a residence card, a derivative residence card, a permanent residence card or a qualifying *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State residence card. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) Before an immigration officer refuses admission to the United Kingdom to a person under this regulation because the person does not produce on arrival a document mentioned in paragraph (1) or (2), the immigration officer must provide every reasonable opportunity for the document to be obtained by, or brought to, the person or allow the person to prove by other means that the person is— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) a family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national with a right to accompany that *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national or join that *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national in the United Kingdom; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) a person who meets the criteria in paragraph (5); or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (d) a family member who has retained the right of residence or a person with a right of permanent residence under regulation 15. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(5) The criteria in this paragraph are that a person (“P”)— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) previously resided in the United Kingdom under regulation 16(3) and would be entitled to reside in the United Kingdom under that regulation were P in the country; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) is accompanying an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national to, or joining an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national in, the United Kingdom and P would be entitled to reside in the United Kingdom under regulation 16(2) were P and the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national both in the United Kingdom; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) is accompanying a person (“the relevant person”) to, or joining the relevant person in, the United Kingdom and— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) the relevant person is residing, or has resided, in the United Kingdom under regulation 16(3); and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] P would be entitled to reside in the United Kingdom under regulation 16(4) were P and the relevant person both in the United Kingdom; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (d) is accompanying a person who meets the criteria in sub-paragraph (b) or (c) (“the relevant person”) to the United Kingdom and— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) P and the relevant person are both— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
    - (aa (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] seeking admission to the United Kingdom in reliance on this paragraph for the first time; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
    - (bb (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] returning to the United Kingdom having previously resided there pursuant to the same provisions of regulation 16 in reliance on which they now base their claim to admission; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] P would be entitled to reside in the United Kingdom under regulation 16(6) were P and the relevant person there; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (e) is accompanying a British citizen to, or joining a British citizen in, the United Kingdom and P would be entitled to reside in the United Kingdom under regulation 16(5) were P and the British citizen both in the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(6) Paragraph (7) applies where— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) a person (“P”) seeks admission to the United Kingdom in reliance on paragraph (5)(b), (c) or (e); and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) if P were in the United Kingdom, P would have a derived right to reside under regulation 16(8)(b)(ii). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(7) Where this paragraph applies a person (“P”) must only be regarded as meeting the criteria in paragraph (5)(b), (c) or (e) where P— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) is accompanying the person with whom P would on admission to the United Kingdom jointly share care responsibility for the purpose of regulation 16(8)(b)(ii); or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) has previously resided in the United Kingdom pursuant to regulation 16(2), (4) or (5) as a joint primary carer and seeks admission to the United Kingdom in order to reside there again on the same basis. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(8) But this regulation is subject to regulations 23(1), (2), (3) and (4) and 31. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(9)[^c-5f86281cb072b1f84213627f5b283956] A person is not entitled to be admitted by virtue of this regulation where that person is subject to a decision under regulation 23(6)(b) (removal decision). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) An *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national is entitled to reside in the United Kingdom for a period not exceeding three months beginning on the date of admission to the United Kingdom provided the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national holds a valid national identity card or passport issued by an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
