### 14. Extended right of residence (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-7fde4d625446b56e4f7c03bf67080094][^c-bbe2b41bfb2d546e51f8080e5f8da0d4][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) A qualified person is entitled to reside in the United Kingdom for as long as that person remains a qualified person. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) A person (“P”) who is a family member of a qualified person residing in the United Kingdom under paragraph (1) or of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national with a right of permanent residence under regulation 15 is entitled to remain in the United Kingdom for so long as P remains the family member of that person or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) A family member who has retained the right of residence is entitled to reside in the United Kingdom for so long as that person remains a family member who has retained the right of residence. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) A person who otherwise satisfies the criteria in this regulation is not entitled to a right to reside in the United Kingdom under this regulation where the Secretary of State or an immigration officer has made a decision under regulation 23(6)(b), 24(1), 25(1), 26(3) or 31(1), *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] or an order under regulation 23(5) (exclusion order) or 32(3) (deportation order), unless that decision or order, as the case may be, is set aside, revoked or otherwise no longer has effect[^c-3c11f5b8ea86012fd959d59aa42be3b8] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137].

### 15. Right of permanent residence (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-5e462c95e746a64c0ecc36fdbf8f7702][^c-d1765f0c10e787961b54f474c77c11c3][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) The following persons acquire the right to reside in the United Kingdom permanently— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national who has resided in the United Kingdom in accordance with these Regulations for a continuous period of five years; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) a family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national who is not an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national but who has resided in the United Kingdom with the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national in accordance with these Regulations for a continuous period of five years; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) a worker or self-employed person who has ceased activity; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (d) the family member of a worker or self-employed person who has ceased activity, provided— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) the person was the family member of the worker or self-employed person at the point the worker or self-employed person ceased activity; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] at that point, the family member enjoyed a right to reside on the basis of being the family member of that worker or self-employed person; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (e) a person who was the family member of a worker or self-employed person where— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) the worker or self-employed person has died; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] the family member resided with the worker or self-employed person immediately before the death; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (iii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] the worker or self-employed person had resided continuously in the United Kingdom for at least two years immediately before dying or the death was the result of an accident at work or an occupational disease; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (f) a person who— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) has resided in the United Kingdom in accordance with these Regulations for a continuous period of five years; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] was, at the end of the period, a family member who has retained the right of residence. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) Residence in the United Kingdom as a result of a derivative right to reside does not constitute residence for the purpose of this regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) The right of permanent residence under this regulation is lost through absence from the United Kingdom for a period exceeding two years. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) A person who satisfies the criteria in this regulation is not entitled to a right to permanent residence in the United Kingdom where the Secretary of State or an immigration officer has made a decision under regulation 23(6)(b), 24(1), 25(1), 26(3) or 31(1), *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] or an order under regulation 23(5) (exclusion order) or 32(3) (deportation order), unless that decision or order, as the case may be, is set aside, revoked or otherwise no longer has effect[^c-0e4982198053ed71c3126737e325d252] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137].

  - (i) is under the age of 18; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

  - (iii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] would be unable to remain in the United Kingdom if the person left the United Kingdom for an indefinite period. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) The criteria in this paragraph are that— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) any of the person’s parents (“ *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]PP *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]”) is an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national who resides or has resided in the United Kingdom; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) both the person and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] PP *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] reside or have resided in the United Kingdom at the same time, and during such a period of residence, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] PP *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] has been a worker in the United Kingdom; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) the person is in education in the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) The criteria in this paragraph are that— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) the person is the primary carer of a person satisfying the criteria in paragraph (3) (“ *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]PPP *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]”); and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) PPP *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] would be unable to continue to be educated in the United Kingdom if the person left the United Kingdom for an indefinite period. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (b) BC is residing in the United Kingdom; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) BC would be unable to reside in the United Kingdom or in another *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State if the person left the United Kingdom for an indefinite period. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(6) The criteria in this paragraph are that— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) the person is under the age of 18; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) the person does not have leave to enter, or remain in, the United Kingdom under the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] (but see paragraph (7A))[^c-a5e8c823dbe991e23d81f7fc1deee927] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137];
- (c) the person’s primary carer is entitled to a derivative right to reside in the United Kingdom under paragraph (2), (4) or (5); and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (d) the primary carer would be prevented from residing in the United Kingdom if the person left the United Kingdom for an indefinite period. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(7) In this regulation— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) “education” excludes nursery education but does not exclude education received before the compulsory school age where that education is equivalent to the education received at or after the compulsory school age; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) “worker” does not include a jobseeker or a person treated as a worker under regulation *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 6(2);
- (c) an “exempt person” is a person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) who has a right to reside under another provision of these Regulations; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] who has the right of abode under section 2 of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00013];
  - (iii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] to whom section 8 of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00014], or an order made under subsection (2) of that section *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00015], applies; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (iv (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] who has indefinite leave to enter or remain in the United Kingdom *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] (but see paragraph (7A))[^c-61c224e6ca9f351accc6bebb4a522c75] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137].

(7A)[^c-c3963e8c45baf85a75d2b5fdbcdd0ab9] Leave to enter, or remain in, the United Kingdom under the 1971 Act which has been granted by virtue of Appendix *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EU *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] to the immigration rules is not to be treated as leave for the purposes of paragraph (6)(b) or (7)(c)(iv). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(8) A person is the “primary carer” of another person (“AP”) if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) the person is a direct relative or a legal guardian of AP; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) either— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) the person has primary responsibility for AP’s care; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] shares equally the responsibility for AP’s care with one other person *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^c-cb72d707e1b61bf172b0b40174597214]....

(9) In paragraph (2)(b)(iii), (4)(b) or (5)(c), if the role of primary carer is shared with another person in accordance with paragraph (8)(b)(ii), the words “the person” are to be read as “both primary carers”. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(10) Paragraph (9) does not apply if the person with whom care responsibility is shared acquired a derivative right to reside in the United Kingdom as a result of this regulation prior to the other person’s assumption of equal care responsibility. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(11) A person is not be regarded as having responsibility for another person’s care for the purpose of paragraph (8) on the sole basis of a financial contribution towards that person’s care. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(12) A person does not have a derivative right to reside where the Secretary of State or an immigration officer has made a decision under regulation 23(6)(b), 24(1), 25(1), 26(3) or 31(1), unless that decision is set aside or otherwise no longer has effect. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

## PART 3 (affected by repeal, see note) — RESIDENCE DOCUMENTATION (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 17. Issue of registration certificate (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d867911917b3f6a900ff99a52a621447][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) The Secretary of State must issue a registration certificate to a qualified person immediately on application and production of— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) a valid national identity card or passport issued by an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) proof that the applicant is a qualified person. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) In the case of a worker, confirmation of the worker’s engagement from the worker’s employer or a certificate of employment is sufficient proof for the purposes of paragraph (1)(b). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) The Secretary of State must issue a registration certificate to an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national who is the family member of a qualified person or of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national with a right of permanent residence under regulation 15 immediately on application and production of— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) a valid national identity card or passport issued by an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) proof that the applicant is such a family member. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) The Secretary of State must issue a registration certificate to an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national who is a family member who has retained the right of residence on application and production of— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) a valid national identity card or passport; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) proof that the applicant is a family member who has retained the right of residence. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(5) The Secretary of State may issue a registration certificate to an extended family member not falling within regulation 7(3) who is an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national on application if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) the application is accompanied or joined by a valid national identity card or passport; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) the relevant *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national is a qualified person or an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national with a right of permanent residence under regulation 15; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) in all the circumstances it appears to the Secretary of State appropriate to issue the registration certificate. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(6) Where the Secretary of State receives an application under paragraph (5) an extensive examination of the personal circumstances of the applicant must be undertaken by the Secretary of State and if the application is refused, the Secretary of State must give reasons justifying the refusal unless this is contrary to the interests of national security. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(7) A registration certificate issued under this regulation must state the name and address of the person registering and the date of registration. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(8) A registration certificate is— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) proof of the holder’s right to reside on the date of issue; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) no longer valid if the holder ceases to have a right to reside under these Regulations; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) invalid if the holder never had a right to reside under these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(9) This regulation is subject to regulations 24 (refusal to issue or renew and revocation of residence documentation) and 25 (cancellation of a right of residence). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 18. Issue of residence card (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-66b0eb520d148fb4a75472261e69b5c4][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) The Secretary of State must issue a residence card to a person who is not an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national and is the family member of a qualified person or of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national with a right of permanent residence under regulation 15 on application and production of— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) a valid passport; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) proof that the applicant is such a family member. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) The Secretary of State must issue a residence card to a person who is not an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national but who is a family member who has retained the right of residence on application and production of— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) a valid passport; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) proof that the applicant is a family member who has retained the right of residence. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) On receipt of an application under paragraph (1) or (2) and the documents that are required to accompany the application the Secretary of State must immediately issue the applicant with a certificate of application for the residence card and the residence card must be issued no later than six months after the date on which the application and documents are received. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) The Secretary of State may issue a residence card to an extended family member not falling within regulation 7(3) who is not an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national on application if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) the application is accompanied or joined by a valid passport; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) the relevant *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national is a qualified person or an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national with a right of permanent residence under regulation 15; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) in all the circumstances it appears to the Secretary of State appropriate to issue the residence card. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(5) Where the Secretary of State receives an application under paragraph (4) an extensive examination of the personal circumstances of the applicant must be undertaken by the Secretary of State and if the application is refused, the Secretary of State must give reasons justifying the refusal unless this is contrary to the interests of national security. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(6) A residence card issued under this regulation is valid for— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) five years from the date of issue; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) in the case of a residence card issued to the family member or extended family member of a qualified person, the envisaged period of residence in the United Kingdom of the qualified person, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

whichever is the shorter. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(7) A residence card— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) must be called “Residence card of a family member of *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] a Union citizen[^c-75876ac4d54c04f72e639bcd4b05a068] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]”;
- (b) is proof of the holder’s right to reside on the date of issue; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) is no longer valid if the holder ceases to have a right to reside under these Regulations; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (d) is invalid if the holder never had a right to reside under these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(8) This regulation is subject to regulations 24 and 25. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 19. Issue of a document certifying permanent residence and a permanent residence card (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-5ad6b07f2e1216a718744624476725c1][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) The Secretary of State must, as soon as possible, issue an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national with a right of permanent residence under regulation 15 with a document certifying permanent residence on application and the production of— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) a valid national identity card or passport issued by an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) proof that the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national has a right of permanent residence. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) The Secretary of State must issue a person who is not an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national who has a right of permanent residence under regulation 15 with a permanent residence card no later than six months after an application is received and the production of— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) a valid passport; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) proof that the person has a right of permanent residence. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) Subject to paragraph (4) a permanent residence card is valid for ten years from the date of issue and must be renewed on application. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) A document certifying permanent residence and a permanent residence card is— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) proof that the holder had a right to reside under regulation 15 on the date of issue; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) no longer valid if the holder ceases to have a right of permanent residence under regulation 15; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) invalid if the holder never had a right of permanent residence under regulation 15. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(5) This regulation is subject to regulations 24 and 25. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) The Secretary of State must issue a person with a derivative residence card on application and on production of— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (b) proof that the applicant has a derivative right to reside under regulation 16. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
