(3) A derivative residence card issued under paragraph (1) is valid until— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) the date five years from the date of issue; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) any earlier date specified by the Secretary of State when issuing the derivative residence card. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) A derivative residence card issued under paragraph (1) must be issued as soon as practicable. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(5) A derivative residence card is— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) proof of the holder’s derivative right to reside on the day of issue; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) no longer valid if the holder ceases to have a derivative right to reside under regulation 16; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) invalid if the holder never had a derivative right to reside under regulation 16. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(6) This regulation is subject to regulations 24 and 25. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 21. Procedure for applications for documentation under this Part and regulation 12 (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-d2992958f033698adb370bdc937ed55d][^c-1aa181d7c943a9ed5e9dddc1af9c2f25][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) An application for documentation under this Part, or for an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] family permit under regulation 12, must be made— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) online, submitted electronically using the relevant pages of www.gov.uk; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) by post or in person, using the relevant application form specified by the Secretary of State on *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] www.gov.uk *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137].

(2) All applications must— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) be accompanied *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] [^c-b1ed9488346d9480d2c5fd882dfb30e3]... by the evidence or proof required by this Part or regulation 12, as the case may be, as well as that required by paragraph *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] (5)[^c-2a55acc8ab13277e8b1e5db533b2763c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137], within the time specified by the Secretary of State on www.gov.uk; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) be complete. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) An application for a residence card or a derivative residence card must be submitted while the applicant is in the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) When an application is submitted otherwise than in accordance with the requirements in this regulation, it is invalid *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] and must be rejected[^c-32114687931183bdf53c3f1bc8da1933] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137].

(4A)[^c-9df9792b90e570a3ef91dc00af38781f] An application for documentation under this Part, or for an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] family permit under regulation 12, is invalid where the person making the application is subject to a removal decision made under regulation 23(6)(b), a deportation order made under regulation 32(3) or an exclusion order made under regulation 23(5). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(5) Where an application for documentation under this Part is made by a person who is not an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national on the basis that the person is or was the family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national or an extended family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national, the application must be accompanied *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] [^c-76d6a7cab20f8d40a97d145a3b971405]... by a valid national identity card or passport in the name of that *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(6) Where— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) there are circumstances beyond the control of an applicant for documentation under this Part; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) as a result, the applicant is unable to comply with the requirements to submit an application online or using the application form specified by the Secretary of State, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

the Secretary of State may accept an application submitted by post or in person which does not use the relevant application form specified by the Secretary of State. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 22. Verification of a right of residence (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-831053a399b355d58921fe0e5ecb35c0][^c-2a8247825509ee9f719fc5cd905d4bd9][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) This regulation applies where the Secretary of State— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) has reasonable doubt as to whether a person (“A”) has a right to reside or a derivative right to reside; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) wants to verify the eligibility of a person (“A”) to apply for an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] family permit or documentation issued under Part 3. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) Where this regulation applies, the Secretary of State may invite A to— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) provide evidence to support the existence of a right to reside or a derivative right to reside (as the case may be), or to support an application for an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] family permit or documentation under this Part; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) attend an interview with the Secretary of State. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) If A purports to have a right to reside on the basis of a relationship with another person (“B”), (including, where B is a British citizen, through having lived with B in another *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State), the Secretary of State may invite B to— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) provide information about their relationship or residence in another *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) attend an interview with the Secretary of State. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) If without good reason A or B (as the case may be)— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) fails to provide the information requested; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) on at least two occasions, fails to attend an interview if so invited; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

the Secretary of State may draw any factual inferences about A’s entitlement to a right to reside as appear appropriate in the circumstances. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(5) The Secretary of State may decide following the drawing of an inference under paragraph (4) that A does not have or ceases to have a right to reside. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(6) But the Secretary of State must not decide that A does not have or ceases to have a right to reside on the sole basis that A failed to comply with this regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(7) This regulation may not be invoked systematically. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

## PART 4 (affected by repeal, see note) — REFUSAL OF ADMISSION AND REMOVAL (affected by repeal, see note) ETC (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 23. Exclusion and removal from the United Kingdom (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-9c6f47e1e031e87a1f7f3ea9d3adb559][^c-af527ec0880aa8a78dca6c4d3a23976a][^c-c6b5f63f1d6ebba4baab711b4718321d][^c-2de0414d6a99b6b7d8d923147eb8e682][^c-1992c182a5d0c3453195698815bc4c0c][^c-95d89315a041653849528af8eb804785][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) A person is not entitled to be admitted to the United Kingdom by virtue of regulation 11 if a refusal to admit that person is justified on grounds of public policy, public security or public health in accordance with regulation 27. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) A person is not entitled to be admitted to the United Kingdom by virtue of regulation 11 if that person is subject to a deportation or exclusion order, except where the person is temporarily admitted pursuant to regulation 41. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) A person is not entitled to be admitted to the United Kingdom by virtue of regulation 11 if the Secretary of State considers there to be reasonable grounds to suspect that the person’s admission would lead to the misuse of a right to reside under regulation 26(1). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) A person is not entitled to be admitted to the United Kingdom as the family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national under regulation 11(2) unless, at the time of arrival— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) that person is accompanying the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national or joining the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national in the United Kingdom; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national has a right to reside. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(5) If the Secretary of State considers that the exclusion of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national or the family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national is justified on the grounds of public policy, public security or public health in accordance with regulation 27 the Secretary of State may make an order prohibiting that person from entering the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(6) Subject to paragraphs (7) and (8), an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national who has entered the United Kingdom or the family member of such a national who has entered the United Kingdom may be removed if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) that person does not have or ceases to have a right to reside under these Regulations; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) the Secretary of State has decided that the person’s removal is justified on grounds of public policy, public security or public health in accordance with regulation 27; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) the Secretary of State has decided that the person’s removal is justified on grounds of misuse of rights under regulation 26(3). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(7) A person must not be removed under paragraph (6)— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) as the automatic consequence of having recourse to the social assistance system of the United Kingdom; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) if that person has leave to remain in the United Kingdom under the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] unless that person’s removal is justified on the grounds of public policy, public security or public health in accordance with regulation 27. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(8) A decision under paragraph (6)(b) must state that upon execution of any deportation order arising from that decision, the person against whom the order was made is prohibited from entering the United Kingdom— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) until the order is revoked; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) for the period specified in the order. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(9) A decision taken under paragraph (6)(b) or (c) has the effect of terminating any right to reside otherwise enjoyed by the individual concerned. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 24. Refusal to issue or renew and revocation of residence documentation (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-66ea14a4d42566ce838c447be62ec3f8][^c-ca3d4f374423caa277ab479b8127d027][^c-25b4d163c65ba0bea8c02a8b810811a2][^c-5b8d8bd2be25caa8496006b8534cfc44][^c-951c236001fab5ef6efe6dd3b4dda27c][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) The Secretary of State may refuse to issue, revoke or refuse to renew a registration certificate, a residence card, a document certifying permanent residence or a permanent residence card if the refusal or revocation is justified on grounds of public policy, public security or public health, or on grounds of misuse of rights in accordance with regulation 26(3). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) A decision under regulation 23(6) or 32(4) to remove a person from the United Kingdom, or a decision under regulation 31 to revoke a person’s admission to the United Kingdom invalidates a registration certificate, residence card, document certifying permanent residence or permanent residence card held by that person or an application made by that person for such a certificate, card or document. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) The Secretary of State may revoke or refuse to renew a registration certificate or a residence card if the holder of the certificate or card has ceased to have, or never had, a right to reside under these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) The Secretary of State may revoke or refuse to renew a document certifying permanent residence or a permanent residence card if the holder of the certificate or card has ceased to have, or never had, a right of permanent residence under regulation 15. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(5) An immigration officer may, at the time of a person’s arrival in the United Kingdom— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) revoke that person’s residence card if the person is not at that time the family member of a qualified person or of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national who has a right of permanent residence under regulation 15, a family member who has retained a right of residence or a person with a right of permanent residence under regulation 15; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) revoke that person’s permanent residence card if the person is not at that time a person with a right of permanent residence under regulation 15. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(6) An entry clearance officer *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] or an immigration officer[^c-4160b513b585db69fc7f6fda108f59c4] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] may at any time revoke a person’s *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] family permit *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137], including one issued in electronic form,[^c-cec44b9077510c9b58441533c2fa9211] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) the revocation is justified on grounds of public policy, public security or public health; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) the person is not at that time the family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national with the right to reside in the United Kingdom under these Regulations or is not accompanying that *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national or joining that *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national in the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(7) Any action taken under this regulation on grounds of public policy, public security or public health must be in accordance with regulation 27. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 25. Cancellation of a right of residence (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-f6812962cf50dde22c30cabc004dcdef][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) Where the conditions in paragraph (2) are met the Secretary of State may cancel a person’s right to reside. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) The conditions in this paragraph are met where— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) a person has a right to reside in the United Kingdom as a result of these Regulations; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) the Secretary of State has decided that the cancellation of that person’s right to reside in the United Kingdom is justified on the grounds of public policy, public security or public health in accordance with regulation 27 or on grounds of misuse of rights in accordance with regulation 26(3); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) the circumstances are such that the Secretary of State cannot make a decision under regulation 24(1); and\` *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (d) it is not possible for the Secretary of State to remove the person from the United Kingdom under regulation 23(6)(b) or (c). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 26. Misuse of a right to reside (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-5a7f020b20fb653c0308d5ef0fd236f9][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) The misuse of a right to reside occurs where a person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) observes the requirements of these Regulations in circumstances which do not achieve the purpose of these Regulations (as determined by reference to Council *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] [Directive 2004/38/EC *(affected by repeal, see note)*](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/eudr/2004/0038)[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00016] and the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EU *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] Treaties); and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) intends to obtain an advantage from these Regulations by engaging in conduct which artificially creates the conditions required to satisfy the criteria set out in these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) Such misuse includes attempting to enter the United Kingdom within 12 months of being removed under regulation 23(6)(a), where the person attempting to do so is unable to provide evidence that, upon re-entry to the United Kingdom, the conditions for a right to reside, other than the initial right of residence under regulation 13, will be met. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) The Secretary of State may take an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] decision on the grounds of misuse of rights where there are reasonable grounds to suspect the misuse of a right to reside and it is proportionate to do so. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) Where, as a result of paragraph (2), the removal of a person under regulation 23(6)(a) may prevent that person from returning to the United Kingdom during the 12 month period following removal, during that 12 month period the person who was removed may apply to the Secretary of State to have the effect of paragraph (2) set aside on the grounds that there has been a material change in the circumstances which justified that person’s removal under regulation 23(6)(a). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(5) An application under paragraph (4) may only be made whilst the applicant is outside the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(6) This regulation may not be invoked systematically. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 27. Decisions taken on grounds of public policy, public security and public health (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-2b828863ebaa02f9163da576a421d9d4][^c-9bb47e646086ed0f8efa793f4aed630d][^c-90b7f7afddda01aa04da7f9110ddc75d][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) In this regulation, a “relevant decision” means an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] decision taken on the grounds of public policy, public security or public health. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) A relevant decision may not be taken to serve economic ends. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) A relevant decision may not be taken in respect of a person with a right of permanent residence under regulation 15 except on serious grounds of public policy and public security. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) A relevant decision may not be taken except on imperative grounds of public security in respect of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national who— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) has a right of permanent residence under regulation 15 and who[^c-b4cb3c3f2672bdf1a5c17ae509c894c0] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] has resided in the United Kingdom for a continuous period of at least ten years prior to the relevant decision; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) is under the age of 18, unless the relevant decision is in the best interests of the person concerned, as provided for in the Convention on the Rights of the Child adopted by the General Assembly of the United Nations on 20th November 1989 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00017].

(5) The public policy and public security requirements of the United Kingdom include restricting rights otherwise conferred by these Regulations in order to protect the fundamental interests of society, and where a relevant decision is taken on grounds of public policy or public security it must also be taken in accordance with the following principles— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) the decision must comply with the principle of proportionality; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) the decision must be based exclusively on the personal conduct of the person concerned; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) the personal conduct of the person must represent a genuine, present and sufficiently serious threat affecting one of the fundamental interests of society, taking into account past conduct of the person and that the threat does not need to be imminent; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (d) matters isolated from the particulars of the case or which relate to considerations of general prevention do not justify the decision; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (e) a person’s previous criminal convictions do not in themselves justify the decision; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (f) the decision may be taken on preventative grounds, even in the absence of a previous criminal conviction, provided the grounds are specific to the person. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(6) Before taking a relevant decision on the grounds of public policy and public security in relation to a person (“P”) who is resident in the United Kingdom, the decision maker must take account of considerations such as the age, state of health, family and economic situation of P, P’s length of residence in the United Kingdom, P’s social and cultural integration into the United Kingdom and the extent of P’s links with P’s country of origin. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(7) In the case of a relevant decision taken on grounds of public health— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) a disease that does not have epidemic potential as defined by the relevant instruments of the World Health Organisation or is not a disease listed in Schedule 1 to the Health Protection (Notification) Regulations 2010 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00018]; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) if the person concerned is in the United Kingdom, any disease occurring after the three month period beginning on the date on which the person arrived in the United Kingdom, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

does not constitute grounds for the decision. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(8) A court or tribunal considering whether the requirements of this regulation are met must (in particular) have regard to the considerations contained in Schedule 1 (considerations of public policy, public security and the fundamental interests of society *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] etc. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]).

### 28. Application of Part 4 to a person with a derivative right to reside (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-abbd3d3eb0781afd57fb0715450b8c27][^c-156073eb30417a8a17677ce42c7a66dd][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) This regulation applies where a person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) would, but for this Part of these Regulations, be entitled to a derivative right to reside (other than a derivative right to reside conferred by regulation 16(3)); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) holds a derivative residence card; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) has applied for a derivative residence card. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) Where this regulation applies, this Part of these Regulations applies as though— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) references to “the family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national” referred instead to “a person with a derivative right to reside”; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) references to a registration certificate, a residence card, a document certifying permanent residence or a permanent residence card referred instead to a “derivative residence card”; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) regulation 24(5) instead conferred on an immigration officer the power to revoke a derivative residence card where the holder is not at that time a person with a derivative right to reside; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (d) regulations 24(4) and 27(3) and (4) were omitted. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

## PART 5 (affected by repeal, see note) — PROCEDURE IN RELATION TO (affected by repeal, see note) EEA (affected by repeal, see note) DECISIONS (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 29. Person claiming right of admission (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0ccb9bf6b42fd38ee01fc923915f92dd][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) This regulation applies to a person who claims a right of admission to the United Kingdom under regulation 11 as— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) a person, not being an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national, who— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) is a family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] is a family member who has retained the right of residence; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (iii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] has a derivative right to reside; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (iv (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] has a right of permanent residence under regulation 15; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (v) is in possession of a qualifying *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State residence card; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national, where there is reason to believe that the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national may be a person to whom regulation 23(1), (2), (3) or (4) applies; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) a person to whom regulation 41 applies (temporary admission to submit case in person). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) A person to whom this regulation applies is to be treated as if that person were a person seeking leave to enter the United Kingdom under the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] for the purposes of paragraphs 2, 3, 4, 7 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] and 16 to 18A[^c-e2a2932d3fec6c4454154ed981cd75ef] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] of Schedule 2 to the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] (administrative provisions as to control on entry *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] etc *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137])[^f00019], except that— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) the reference in paragraph 2(1) to the purpose for which the immigration officer may examine any persons who have arrived in the United Kingdom is to be read as a reference to the purpose of determining whether the person is to be granted admission under these Regulations; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) the references in paragraphs 3, 7 and 16(1) to a person who is, or may be, given leave to enter are to be read as references to a person who is, or may be, granted admission under these Regulations; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) a medical examination is not to be carried out under paragraph 2 or paragraph 7 as a matter of routine and may only be carried out within three months of the person’s arrival in the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3)[^c-6d777c391390f81a55c70731b969cdf9] For so long as a person to whom this regulation applies is detained under the powers conferred by Schedule 2 to the 1971 Act, or granted bail under Schedule 10 to the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2016 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] whilst liable to be detained under the powers conferred by Schedule 2 to the 1971 Act, the person is deemed not to have been admitted to the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 30. Person refused admission (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-1c96e05cfce30e0c2ecd94178cebd413][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) This regulation applies to a person who is in the United Kingdom and has been refused admission to the United Kingdom— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) because that person does not meet the requirements of regulation 11 (including where that person does not meet those requirements because that person’s *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] family permit, residence card, derivative residence card or permanent residence card has been revoked by an immigration officer in accordance with regulation 24); or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) in accordance with regulation 23(1), (2), (3) or (4). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) A person to whom this regulation applies, is to be treated as if the person were a person refused leave to enter under the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] for the purpose of paragraphs 8, 10, 10A, 11 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] and 16 to 19[^c-1a4725e3a584765e38607879b93635b9] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] of Schedule 2 to the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00020] (and the provisions of Schedule 10 to the[^c-cfb0e7cf01abb4373bba350f35bbf812] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2016 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] apply accordingly) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137], except that the reference in paragraph 19 to a certificate of entitlement, entry clearance or work permit is to be read as a reference to an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] family permit, residence card, derivative residence card, a qualifying *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State residence card, or a permanent residence card. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 31. Revocation of admission (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-2cde9a53af142248715147dbf1298f8f][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) This regulation applies to a person admitted to the United Kingdom under regulation 11 in circumstances where, under regulation 23(1), (2) or (3) that person was not entitled to be admitted. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) Paragraph 6(2) of Schedule 2 to the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] (administrative provisions as to control on entry: refusal of leave to enter) applies to a person to whom this regulation applies, as though the references: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) to that person’s examination under paragraph 2 of Schedule 2 to the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] were to that paragraph as applied by regulation 29(2)(a) and (c); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) to notices of leave to enter the United Kingdom were to a decision to admit that person to the United Kingdom under these Regulations; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) to the cancellation of such a notice and the refusal of leave to enter were to revocation of the decision to admit that person to the United Kingdom under this regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) Where a person’s admission to the United Kingdom is revoked, that person is to be treated as a person to whom admission to the United Kingdom has been refused and regulation 30 applies accordingly. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 32. Person subject to removal (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-15257e72a38673376ef27edde6f60475][^c-27ffd4af434b97ded119cb43e0bf509e][^c-f231afbe25bfeaf64c799169523fb915][^c-4e1c49b49bcc68b9022b1be191a2d105][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) If there are reasonable grounds for suspecting that a person is someone who may be removed from the United Kingdom under regulation 23(6)(b), that person may be detained under the authority of the Secretary of State pending a decision whether or not to remove the person under that regulation, and paragraphs 17 to 18A of Schedule 2 to the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] apply in relation to the detention of such a person as those paragraphs apply in relation to a person who may be detained under paragraph 16 of that Schedule. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) Where a decision is taken to remove a person under regulation 23(6)(a) or (c), the person is to be treated as if the person were a person to whom section 10(1) of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1999 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00021] applies, and section 10 of that Act (removal of certain persons unlawfully in the United Kingdom) is to apply accordingly. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) Where a decision is taken to remove a person under regulation 23(6)(b), the person is to be treated as if the person were a person to whom section 3(5)(a) of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00022] (liability to deportation) applies, and section 5 of that Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00023] (procedure for deportation) and Schedule 3 to that Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00024] (supplementary provision as to deportation) are to apply accordingly. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) A person who enters the United Kingdom in breach of a deportation or exclusion order, or in circumstances where that person was not entitled to be admitted under regulation 23(1) or (3), is removable as an illegal entrant under Schedule 2 to the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] and the provisions of that Schedule apply accordingly. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(5) Where a deportation order is made against a person but the person is not removed under the order during the two year period beginning on the date on which the order is made, the Secretary of State may only take action to remove the person under the order at the end of that period if, having assessed whether there has been any material change in circumstances since the deportation order was made, the Secretary of State considers that the removal continues to be justified on the grounds of public policy, public security or public health. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(6) A person to whom this regulation applies must be allowed one month to leave the United Kingdom, beginning on the date on which the decision to remove is communicated before being removed because of that decision except— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) in duly substantiated cases of urgency; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) where the person is detained pursuant to the sentence or order of any court; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) where the person is a person to whom paragraph (4) applies. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(7) Paragraph (6) does not apply where a decision has been taken under regulation 23(6) on the basis that the relevant person— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) has ceased to have a derivative right to reside; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) is a person who would have had a derivative right to reside but for the effect of a decision to remove under regulation 23(6)(b). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 33. Human rights considerations and interim orders to suspend removal (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-7868623a741262ca4f9f155294e5628a][^c-307007526016846a89a621e250336e09][^c-c192af6ae6fe6fe807bb5555b8133597][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) This regulation applies where the Secretary of State intends to give directions for the removal of a person (“P”) to whom regulation 32(3) applies, in circumstances where— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) P has not appealed against the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] decision to which regulation 32(3) applies, but would be entitled, and remains within time, to do so from within the United Kingdom (ignoring any possibility of an appeal out of time with permission); or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) P has so appealed but the appeal has not been finally determined. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) The Secretary of State may only give directions for P’s removal if the Secretary of State certifies that, despite the appeals process not having been begun or not having been finally determined, removal of P to the country or territory to which P is proposed to be removed, pending the outcome of P’s appeal, would not be unlawful under section 6 of the Human Rights Act 1998 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00025] (public authority not to act contrary to Human Rights Convention). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) The grounds upon which the Secretary of State may certify a removal under paragraph (2) include (in particular) that P would not, before the appeal is finally determined, face a real risk of serious irreversible harm if removed to the country or territory to which P is proposed to be removed. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) If P applies to the appropriate court or tribunal (whether by means of judicial review or otherwise) for an interim order to suspend enforcement of the removal decision, P may not be removed from the United Kingdom until such time as the decision on the interim order has been taken, except— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) where the removal decision is based on a previous judicial decision; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) where P has had previous access to judicial review; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) where the removal decision is based on imperative grounds of public security. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(5) In this regulation, “finally determined” has the same meaning as in Part 6. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 34. Revocation of deportation and exclusion orders (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-bc336afec0a6c658acb39615e7866a25][^c-9ee9778e585545cf643bf5a40fd8ac3d][^c-93d5f5d0bb885abef5d191fecd73b90e][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) An exclusion order remains in force unless it is revoked by the Secretary of State under this regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) A deportation order remains in force— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) until the order is revoked under this regulation; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) for the period specified in the order. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) A person who is subject to a deportation or exclusion order may only apply to the Secretary of State to have it revoked on the basis that there has been a material change in the circumstances that justified the making of the order. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) An application under paragraph (3) must set out the material change in circumstances relied upon by the applicant and may only be made whilst the applicant is outside the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(5) On receipt of an application under paragraph (3), the Secretary of State must revoke the order if the Secretary of State considers that the criteria for making such an order are no longer satisfied. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(6) The Secretary of State must take a decision on an application under paragraph (2) no later than six months after the date on which the application is received. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

## PART 6 (affected by repeal, see note) — APPEALS UNDER THESE REGULATIONS (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 35. Interpretation of Part 6 (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0ca12023ec70ee8a7d7d9e6334eeb53a][^c-0c228114d9465e420e0829744077f8f3][^c-bb407a670df28f9daeb92d8d3210322f][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) In this Part— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- “the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1997 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]” means the Special Immigration Appeals Commission Act 1997 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00026];
- “Commission” has the same meaning as in the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1997 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137].

(2) For the purposes of this Part, and subject to paragraphs (3) and (4), an appeal is to be treated as pending during the period when notice of appeal is given and ending when the appeal is finally determined, withdrawn or abandoned. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) An appeal is not to be treated as finally determined while a further appeal may be brought; and, if such a further appeal is brought, the original appeal is not to be treated as finally determined until the further appeal is determined, withdrawn or abandoned. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) A pending appeal is not to be treated as abandoned solely because the appellant leaves the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 36. Appeal rights (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-7296b2a46ebefdf09efc9bda28c73c37][^c-4f3d121bf7eeb45aa8925da5fc2ae8d3][^c-95640be31d4fcdc76efdf032276ecc97][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) The subject of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] decision may appeal against that decision under these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) If a person claims to be an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national, that person may not appeal under these Regulations without producing a valid national identity card or passport issued by an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) If a person claims to be in a durable relationship with an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national, that person may not appeal under these Regulations without producing— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) a valid passport; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) either— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] family permit; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] sufficient evidence to satisfy the Secretary of State that the person is in a relationship with the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) If a person to whom paragraph (2) does not apply claims to be the family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national under regulation 7, the relative of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national who is an extended family member under regulation 8, or a family member who has retained the right of residence under regulation 10, that person may not appeal under these Regulations without producing— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) a valid passport; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) either— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] family permit *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] a qualifying *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State residence card; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (iii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] in the case of a person claiming to be the family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] proof that the definition of “family member” in regulation 7(1) is met[^c-fcd7bef5127e90cc77d079f72a07bfd2] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]; [^c-7fff29ce0f29de1343de332cf6e83ecd]...
  - (iv (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] in the case of a person claiming to be a family member who has retained the right of residence, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] proof that the definition of “family member who has retained the right of residence” in regulation 10(1) is met; or[^c-51062433f6d5590996cfb3ebbd563d13] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (v)[^c-6ef0883c777164aed4eb00f24254c8df] in the case of a person claiming to be the relative of an EEA national who is an extended family member, proof that the definition of “extended family member” in regulation 8(1) is met. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(5) If a person (“P”) claims to have a derivative right to reside, P may not appeal under these Regulations unless P produces a valid national identity card issued by an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State or a valid passport, and either— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] family permit; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) where P claims to have a derivative right to reside as a result of— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) regulation 16(2), proof that P is a direct relative or legal guardian of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national who is under the age of 18; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] regulation 16(3), proof that P is the child of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (iii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] regulation 16(4), proof that P is a direct relative or legal guardian of the child of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (iv (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] regulation 16(5), proof that P is a direct relative or legal guardian of a British citizen; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (v) regulation 16(6), proof that P is under the age of 18 and is a dependant of a person satisfying the criteria in paragraph (i), (iii) or (iv). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(6) If a person claims to be entitled to a right to reside under regulation 9 (family members *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] or extended family members[^c-8f358b1ecb1bc5bec655780344732046] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] of British citizens), that person may not appeal without producing a valid passport and either— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] family permit; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) in respect of a family member,[^c-86a382ec4aabaaf51a890c298b7a8258] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] a qualifying *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State residence card; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) proof that the criteria to be a family member of the British citizen are met; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] proof that the British citizen is residing, or did reside, in another *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State as a worker, self-employed person, self-sufficient person or student. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c)[^c-b0a0ed5c0d252a0a964fcc74384751b8] in respect of an extended family member— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i)[^c-d415f7b826c93d94c6817cc055f4b8e9] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] proof that— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
    - (aa (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] the condition in regulation 9(1A)(b) is met; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
    - (bb (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] the criteria to be *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] an extended family member[^c-0eac3b218248cfee42120f82eb24f4ef] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] of the British citizen are met, and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
    - (cc (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] the British citizen is residing, or did reside, in another *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State as a worker, self-employed person, self-sufficient person or a student. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(7) The Secretary of State or an immigration officer may certify a ground for the purposes of paragraph (8) if it has been considered in a previous appeal brought under these Regulations or under section 82(1) of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2002 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00027] .

(9) Except where an appeal lies to the Commission, an appeal under these Regulations lies to the First-tier Tribunal. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(12)[^c-f8a975f6387490e6a21e71abe8b69969] Where there is a requirement under this regulation to produce an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] family permit— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) where notice of appeal is given electronically, the permit may be produced either in paper or electronic form; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) in all other cases, the permit must be produced in paper form. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 37. Out of country appeals (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-c7de140055c489b3f5e6d3fd41464aec][^c-923eb53ad4892d5a8c56e250b1255ce5][^c-aef7628c4765cfc0524f8943274adbe0][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) Subject to paragraph (2), a person may not appeal under regulation 36 whilst in the United Kingdom against an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] decision— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) to refuse to admit that person to the United Kingdom; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) to revoke that person’s admission to the United Kingdom; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) to make an exclusion order against that person; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (d) to refuse to revoke a deportation or exclusion order made against the person; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (e) to refuse to issue the person with an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] family permit; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (f) to revoke, or to refuse to issue or renew any document under these Regulations where that decision is taken at a time when the person is outside the United Kingdom; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (g) to remove the person from the United Kingdom following entry to the United Kingdom in breach of a deportation or exclusion order, or in circumstances where that person was not entitled to be admitted pursuant to regulation 23(1), (2), (3) or (4). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) Sub-paragraphs (a) to (c) of paragraph (1) do not apply where the person is in the United Kingdom and— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a)[^c-d12741b1c330bf8455cb8a6fad8ce5b3] the person, not being a person who is deemed not to have been admitted to the United Kingdom under regulation 29(3)— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) holds a valid *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] family permit, registration certificate, residence card, derivative residence card, document certifying permanent residence, permanent residence card or qualifying *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State residence card on arrival in the United Kingdom; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] can otherwise prove that the person is resident in the United Kingdom; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) the person is deemed not to have been admitted to the United Kingdom under regulation 29(3) but at the date on which notice of the decision to refuse admission is given the person has been in the United Kingdom for at least 3 months. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) An appeal against an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] decision lies to the Commission where paragraph (2) or (4) applies. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) in the interests of national security; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) in the interests of national security; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) in the interests of the relationship between the United Kingdom and another country; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) otherwise in the public interest. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(6) Where a certificate is issued under paragraph (2) or (4) in respect of a pending appeal to the First-tier Tribunal or Upper Tribunal the appeal must lapse. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(9) Where the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1997 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] applies to an appeal to the Commission under this regulation, section 2(2) of that Act is to be treated as though it applies the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2002 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] to that appeal in the form modified by Schedule 2 to these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 40. Effect of appeals to the First-tier Tribunal or Upper Tribunal (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-552f586a22f18503f786872c474f9484][^c-9036f1f7004ea56515220bb5ccc7c69d][^c-acc6774f854e1734920297df458f3e39][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) This regulation applies to appeals under these Regulations made to the First-tier Tribunal or Upper Tribunal. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) If a person in the United Kingdom appeals against an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] decision refusing admission to the United Kingdom (other than a decision under regulation 23(1), (2), or (5)), any directions for that person’s removal from the United Kingdom previously given by virtue of the refusal cease to have effect, except in so far as they have already been carried out, while the appeal is pending. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) If a person in the United Kingdom appeals against an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] decision concerning that person’s removal from the United Kingdom (other than a decision under regulation 23(6)(b)), any directions for removal given under section 10 of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1999 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] or Schedule 3 to the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] are to have no effect, except in so far as they have already been carried out, while the appeal is pending. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) The provisions of Part 1 of Schedule 2, or as the case may be, Schedule 3 to the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] concerning detention and persons liable to detention, apply to a person appealing against a refusal of admission, a decision to revoke admission, or a removal decision as if there were in force directions for that person’s removal from the United Kingdom, except that the person may not be detained on board a ship or aircraft so as to compel that person to leave the United Kingdom while the appeal is pending *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] and the provisions of Schedule 10 to the[^c-fbcc82962ec287e3a4b2ea21f6ff0d78] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2016 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] apply accordingly *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137].

(5) In paragraph (4), the words “except that the person” to the end do not apply to an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] decision to which regulation 33 applies (human rights considerations and interim orders to suspend removal). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(6) In calculating the period of two months limited by paragraph 8(2) of Schedule 2 to the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] for— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) the giving of directions under that paragraph for the removal of a person from the United Kingdom; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) the giving of a notice of intention to give such directions, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

any period during which there is an appeal pending by that person is to be disregarded (except in cases where the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] decision was taken under regulation 23(1), (2), (5) and (6)(b). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(7)[^c-c8021cb21cf52696b2933b704ce02239] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

### 41. Temporary admission to submit case in person (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-040d656dd4c14b676bc726aecea5ef4c][^c-ad5648d0e32199e0f05382d688e07a12][^c-0cd1f73b4ca8336040c0b5512d953ff5][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) This regulation applies where— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) a person (“P”) is subject to a decision to remove made under regulation 23(6)(b); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) P has appealed against the decision referred to in sub-paragraph (a); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) a date for P’s appeal has been set by the First-tier Tribunal or Upper Tribunal; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (d) P wants to make submissions before the First-tier Tribunal or Upper Tribunal in person; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (e) P is outside the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) P may apply to the Secretary of State for permission to be temporarily admitted *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] [^c-0941f2a04968d47ea046412d5f07e45b]... to the United Kingdom in order to make submissions in person. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) The Secretary of State must grant P permission, except when P’s appearance may cause serious troubles to public policy or public security. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) When determining when P is entitled to be given permission, and the duration of P’s temporary admission should permission be granted, the Secretary of State must have regard to the dates upon which P will be required to make submissions in person. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(5) Where— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) P is temporarily admitted to the United Kingdom pursuant to this regulation; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) a hearing of P’s appeal has taken place; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) the appeal is not finally determined, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

P may be removed from the United Kingdom pending the remaining stages of the appeal (but P may apply to return to the United Kingdom to make submissions in person during the remaining stages of the appeal in accordance with this regulation). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(6) Where the Secretary of State grants P permission to be temporarily admitted to the United Kingdom under this regulation, upon such admission P is to be treated as if P were a person refused leave to enter under the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] for the purposes of paragraphs 8, 10, 10A, 11 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] and 16 to 18A[^c-03d0e64175d91786d70e9bf5d65f58e0] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] of Schedule 2 to the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] and the provisions of Schedule 10 to the[^c-e3cfe3a56e9778c7cfc7aef66baf420d] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2016 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] apply accordingly *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137].

(7) Where Schedule 2 to the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] so applies, it has effect as if— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) the reference in paragraph 8(1) to leave to enter were a reference to admission to the United Kingdom under these Regulations; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) the reference in paragraph 16(1) to detention pending a decision regarding leave to enter or remain in the United Kingdom were to detention pending submission of P’s case in person in accordance with this regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(8) P is deemed not to have been admitted to the United Kingdom during any time during which P is temporarily admitted pursuant to this regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(9)[^c-b522c749bb8cd44a236731e9c07938e3] For the purposes of this regulation, a person (“P”) is temporarily admitted to the United Kingdom if P is admitted on bail under Schedule 10 to the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2016 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] without having otherwise been admitted, and the expression temporary admission is to be construed accordingly. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) Subject to paragraph (2), where a provision of these Regulations requires a person to hold or produce a valid national identity card issued by an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State or a valid passport, the Secretary of State may accept alternative evidence of identity and nationality where the person is unable to obtain or produce the required document due to circumstances beyond the person’s control. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
