## SCHEDULE 1 (affected by repeal, see note) — CONSIDERATIONS OF PUBLIC POLICY, PUBLIC SECURITY AND THE FUNDAMENTAL INTERESTS OF SOCIETY (affected by repeal, see note) ETC. (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-7b5fc35527525988cca56edd301fc9c8][^c-3dac697fe7b7c50bf5950480d98cfb5d][^c-abdd6035feb5602e2db22abe111caf58][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

Regulation 27 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 1. Considerations of public policy and public security (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-7b5fc35527525988cca56edd301fc9c8][^c-3dac697fe7b7c50bf5950480d98cfb5d][^c-abdd6035feb5602e2db22abe111caf58][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

The *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EU *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] Treaties do not impose a uniform scale of public policy or public security values: member States enjoy considerable discretion, acting within the parameters set by the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EU *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] Treaties, applied where relevant by the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] agreement, to define their own standards of public policy and public security, for purposes tailored to their individual contexts, from time to time. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 2. Application of paragraph 1 to the United Kingdom (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-7b5fc35527525988cca56edd301fc9c8][^c-3dac697fe7b7c50bf5950480d98cfb5d][^c-abdd6035feb5602e2db22abe111caf58][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

An *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national or the family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national having extensive familial and societal links with persons of the same nationality or language does not amount to integration in the United Kingdom; a significant degree of wider cultural and societal integration must be present before a person may be regarded as integrated in the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

**3.** Where an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national or the family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national has received a custodial sentence, or is a persistent offender, the longer the sentence, or the more numerous the convictions, the greater the likelihood that the individual’s continued presence in the United Kingdom represents a genuine, present and sufficiently serious threat affecting of the fundamental interests of society. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

**4.** Little weight is to be attached to the integration of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national or the family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national within the United Kingdom if the alleged integrating links were formed at or around the same time as— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) the commission of a criminal offence; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) an act otherwise affecting the fundamental interests of society; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national or family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national was in custody. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

**5.** The removal from the United Kingdom of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national or the family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national who is able to provide substantive evidence of not demonstrating a threat (for example, through demonstrating that the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national or the family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national has successfully reformed or rehabilitated) is less likely to be proportionate. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

**6.** It is consistent with public policy and public security requirements in the United Kingdom that *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] decisions may be taken in order to refuse, terminate or withdraw any right otherwise conferred by these Regulations in the case of abuse of rights or fraud, including— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) entering, attempting to enter or assisting another person to enter or to attempt to enter, a marriage, civil partnership or durable partnership of convenience; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) fraudulently obtaining or attempting to obtain, or assisting another to obtain or to attempt to obtain, a right to reside under these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 7. The fundamental interests of society (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-7b5fc35527525988cca56edd301fc9c8][^c-3dac697fe7b7c50bf5950480d98cfb5d][^c-abdd6035feb5602e2db22abe111caf58][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

For the purposes of these Regulations, the fundamental interests of society in the United Kingdom include— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) preventing unlawful immigration and abuse of the immigration laws, and maintaining the integrity and effectiveness of the immigration control system (including under these Regulations) and of the Common Travel Area; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) maintaining public order; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) preventing social harm; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (d) preventing the evasion of taxes and duties; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (e) protecting public services; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (f) excluding or removing an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national or family member of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national with a conviction (including where the conduct of that person is likely to cause, or has in fact caused, public offence) and maintaining public confidence in the ability of the relevant authorities to take such action; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (g) tackling offences likely to cause harm to society where an immediate or direct victim may be difficult to identify but where there is wider societal harm (such as offences related to the misuse of drugs or crime with a cross-border dimension as mentioned in Article 83(1) of the Treaty on the Functioning of the European Union); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (h) combating the effects of persistent offending (particularly in relation to offences, which if taken in isolation, may otherwise be unlikely to meet the requirements of regulation 27); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (i) protecting the rights and freedoms of others, particularly from exploitation and trafficking; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (j) protecting the public; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (k) acting in the best interests of a child (including where doing so entails refusing a child admission to the United Kingdom, or otherwise taking an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] decision against a child); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (l) countering terrorism and extremism and protecting shared values. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

## SCHEDULE 2 (affected by repeal, see note) — APPEALS TO THE FIRST-TIER TRIBUNAL (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-4b0aeeb8af6fe1bb8dcfed5d70f3684d][^c-57bd21465a639b1ec5a66654f2b1213f][^c-2a22b9159ddd9e3338b00c1eb7d1475b][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

Regulation 36 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

**1.** The following provisions of, or made under, the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2002 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] have effect in relation to an appeal under these Regulations to the First-tier Tribunal as if it were an appeal against a decision of the Secretary of State under section 82(1) of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2002 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] (right of appeal to the Tribunal)— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- section 84 (grounds of appeal) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00031], as though the sole permitted grounds of appeal were that the decision breaches the appellant’s rights under the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EU *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] Treaties in respect of entry to or residence in the United Kingdom (“an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EU *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] ground of appeal”); *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- section 85 (matters to be considered) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00032], as though— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (a) the references to a statement under section 120 of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2002 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00033] include, but are not limited to, a statement under that section as applied by paragraph 2; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (b) a “matter” in subsection (2) and a “new matter” in subsection (6) include a ground of appeal of a kind listed in section 84 of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2002 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] and an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EU *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] ground of appeal; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- section 86 (determination of appeal) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00034];
- section 105 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00035] and any regulations made under that section; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- section 106 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00036] and any rules made pursuant to that section. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

**1A.**[^c-83dd184a1bdea7f0b8eb2266393f4027] Section 86B of the 2002 Act (timeframe for determination of appeal brought by certain non-detained appellants liable to deportation) applies in relation to an appeal under these Regulations to the First-tier Tribunal as it applies in relation to an appeal under section 82(1) of the 2002 Act but as if for subsection (4) there were substituted— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

> (4) But subsection (3) does not apply or, as the case may be, ceases to apply, if the appeal is brought, or is continued, from outside the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

**2.** (1) Section 92(3) of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2002 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00037] has effect as though an additional basis upon which an appeal under section 82(1)(b) of that Act (human rights claim appeal) must be brought from outside the United Kingdom were that— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) the claim to which that appeal relates arises from an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] decision or the consequences of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] decision; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) the removal of that person from the United Kingdom has been certified under regulation 33 (human rights considerations and interim orders to suspend removal). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) Section 120 of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2002 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] applies to a person (“P”) if an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] decision has been taken or may be taken in respect of P and, accordingly, the Secretary of State or an immigration officer may by notice require a statement from P under subsection (2) of that section, and that notice has effect for the purpose of section 96(2) of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2002 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00038].

(3) Where section 120 of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2002 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] so applies, it has effect as though— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) subsection (3) also provides that a statement under subsection (2) need not repeat reasons or grounds relating to the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] decision under challenge previously advanced by P; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) subsection (5) also applies where P does not have a right to reside. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) For the purposes of an appeal brought under section 82(1) of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2002 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137], subsections (2) and (6)(a) of section 85 (matters to be considered) have effect as though section 84 included a ground of appeal that the decision appealed against breaches the appellant’s right under the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EU *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] Treaties in respect of entry into or residence in the United Kingdom. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

**3.** Tribunal Procedure Rules made under section 22 of the Tribunals, Courts and Enforcement Act 2007 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00039] have effect in relation to appeals under these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

## SCHEDULE 3 (affected by repeal, see note) — EFFECT ON OTHER LEGISLATION (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-7509ff903edde15ad5766797c6ae3f80][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

Regulation 43 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 1. Leave under the (affected by repeal, see note) 1971 Act (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-7509ff903edde15ad5766797c6ae3f80][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

Where a person has leave to enter or remain under the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] which is subject to conditions and that person also has a right to reside under these Regulations, those conditions do not have effect for as long as the person has that right to reside. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 2. Person not subject to restriction on the period for which they may remain (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-7509ff903edde15ad5766797c6ae3f80][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) For the purposes of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] and British Nationality Act 1981, a person who has a right of permanent residence under regulation 15 must be regarded as a person who is in the United Kingdom without being subject under the immigration laws to any restriction on the period for which the person may remain. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) But a qualified person, the family member of a qualified person, a person with a derivative right to reside and a family member who has retained the right of residence must not, by virtue of that status, be so regarded for those purposes. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 3. Carriers’ liability under the (affected by repeal, see note) 1999 Act (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-7509ff903edde15ad5766797c6ae3f80][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

For the purposes of satisfying a requirement to produce a visa under section 40(1)(b) of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1999 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] (charges in respect of passenger without proper documents) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00040], “a visa of the required kind” includes an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] family permit, a residence card, a derivative residence card, a qualifying *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State residence card, or a permanent residence card required for admission under regulation 11(2), or permission to be temporarily admitted under regulation 41. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### PART 2 (affected by repeal, see note) — Savings and modifications (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-1eaaa35efa1f77c04de717b3fe8d90ab][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

#### 2. Accession member States: savings and modifications (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) Regulations 7A and 7B of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2006 Regulations *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] (arrangements for accession member States) continue to have effect in relation to any *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national to whom they applied immediately before 1st February 2017. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) Where regulations 7A and 7B continue to have effect— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) they do so with the following modifications— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) in paragraph (3) of regulation 7A and paragraph (4) of regulation 7B, as though the references to treating periods of involuntary unemployment duly recorded by the relevant employment office as periods of work for the purposes of regulation 5(7)(c) of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2006 Regulations *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] were to treating such periods of involuntary unemployment as periods of work for the purposes of regulation 6(2) of these Regulations; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] as though the references to regulations 6(2) (persons who continue to be treated as a worker) and 15 (right of permanent residence) were references to those provisions in these Regulations; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) these Regulations have effect save that regulation 17 (issue of registration certificate) has effect as though, in paragraph (9), for “regulation 24” there were substituted “regulations 7A and 7B of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2006 Regulations *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] and regulation 24 of these Regulations”. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

#### 3. Appeals (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-a1c26c119625413d9190ac3eca2af813][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) Notwithstanding the revocation of the 2006 Regulations by paragraph 1(1), those Regulations continue to apply— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) in respect of an appeal under those Regulations against an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] decision which is pending (within the meaning of regulation 25(2) of the 2006 Regulations) on 31st January 2017; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) in a case where a person has, on 31st January 2017, a right under those Regulations to appeal against an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] decision. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) For the purposes of this paragraph, “ *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] decision” has the meaning given in regulation 2 of the 2006 Regulations and the definition of “ *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] decision” in regulation 2 of these Regulations does not apply. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

## SCHEDULE 6 (affected by repeal, see note) — TRANSITIONAL PROVISIONS (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-3f0f3dc2c4daedb782106bc863bd4af6][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 1. Interpretation (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-3f0f3dc2c4daedb782106bc863bd4af6][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) In this Schedule, “permission to be temporarily admitted in order to make submissions in person” means— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) in relation to the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2006 Regulations *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137], permission to be temporarily admitted under regulation 29AA(2) of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2006 Regulations *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137];
- (b) in relation to these Regulations, permission to be temporarily admitted under regulation 41(2). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) References to documents applied for or issued under the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2006 Regulations *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] are to those documents as defined in regulation 2(1) of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2006 Regulations *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137].

(1) An *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] family permit issued under regulation 12 of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2006 Regulations *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] before 1st February 2017 is to be treated as an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] family permit issued under regulation 12 of these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) Nothing in this paragraph extends the validity of any document issued under the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2006 Regulations *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] beyond that document’s original period of validity. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

made but not determined before 1st February 2017 is to be treated as having been made under these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(5) A person removed under regulation 19(3)(a) of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2006 Regulations *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] before 1st February 2017 is to be taken into account for the purposes of regulation 26(2). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

### 6. Certification under regulations 24AA and 29AA of the (affected by repeal, see note) 2006 Regulations (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-3f0f3dc2c4daedb782106bc863bd4af6][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(1) Any period of time during which an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national (“P”) resided in the United Kingdom in accordance with the conditions listed in sub-paragraphs (2) or (3) is to be taken into account for the purpose of calculating periods of residence in the United Kingdom in accordance with these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) the Immigration (European Economic Area) Regulations 2000 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137][^f00041]; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2006 Regulations *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137].

(3) The condition in this paragraph is that P resided in the United Kingdom in circumstances where— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) P was a national of a State which at that time was not an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) P had leave to enter or remain in the United Kingdom under the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 1971 Act *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] for the duration of P’s residence; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (c) P would have been residing in the United Kingdom in accordance with these Regulations, had P’s State of origin been an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] State at that time, and had these Regulations been in force. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) Any period during which P resided in the United Kingdom in circumstances which met the conditions in sub-paragraph (2) or (3) is not to be taken into account for the purposes of sub-paragraph (1) where that residence was followed by a period of at least two continuous years during which— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) P was absent from the United Kingdom; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) P’s residence in the United Kingdom— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) did not meet the conditions in sub-paragraph (2) or (3); or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] was not otherwise in accordance with these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (c) any of the criteria in sub-paragraphs (2), (3) and (4) is met; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

P will, notwithstanding the effect of the definition of an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national in regulation 2, be regarded as an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national for the purpose of these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) The criterion in this sub-paragraph is met where F was on 16th July 2012 a person with the right of permanent residence in the United Kingdom under the 2006 Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(3) Subject to sub-paragraph (5), the criterion in this sub-paragraph is met where F— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) was on 16th July 2012 a person with a right of residence in the United Kingdom under the 2006 Regulations; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) on 16th October 2012— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (i) held a valid registration certificate or residence card issued under the 2006 Regulations; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (ii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] had made an application under the 2006 Regulations for a registration certificate or residence card which had not been determined; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
  - (iii (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] had made an application under the 2006 Regulations for a registration certificate or residence card which had been refused and in respect of which an appeal under regulation 26 of the 2006 Regulations could be brought while the appellant was in the United Kingdom (excluding the possibility of an appeal out of time with permission) or was pending (within the meaning of section 104 of the Nationality, Immigration and Asylum Act 2002, as it applied on 16th July 2012). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(4) Subject to sub-paragraph (6), the criterion in this sub-paragraph is met where F— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) had, prior to 16th July 2012, applied for an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] family permit pursuant to regulation 12 of the 2006 Regulations; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) had applied for and been refused an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] family permit and where, on 16th July 2012, an appeal under regulation 26 of the 2006 Regulations against that decision could be brought (excluding the possibility of an appeal out of time with permission) or was pending (within the meaning of section 104 of the Nationality, Immigration and Asylum Act 2002 Act, as it applied on 16th July 2012). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(5) The criterion in sub-paragraph (3) is not met in a case to which sub-paragraph (3)(b)(ii) or (iii) applies where no registration certificate or residence card was, in fact, issued pursuant to that application. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(6) The criterion in sub-paragraph (4) is not met where— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) F was issued with an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] family permit pursuant to an application made prior to 16th July 2012 but F had not been admitted to the United Kingdom within six months of the date on which it was issued; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) no *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] family permit was, in fact, issued pursuant to that application. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(7) Where met, the criteria in sub-paragraphs (2), (3) and (4) remain satisfied until the occurrence of the earliest of the following events— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

- (a) the date on which F ceases to be the family member of P; or *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]
- (b) the date on which F’s right of permanent residence is lost. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(8) P will only continue to be regarded as an *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] EEA *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] national for the purpose of considering the position of F under these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

**1.**[^c-a6d3466dd2afe7519ba16cfb393b8e31][^c-539970c8244842cc4dd08969cccbda3d] (1) Unless the context otherwise requires— *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

  - (i) any reference to a provision of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2006 Regulations *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] in column 1 of the table has effect as though it were a reference to the corresponding provision of these Regulations listed in column 2; and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

(2) Unless otherwise specified in the table, sub-divisions of the provisions of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] 2006 Regulations *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137] listed in column 1 correspond to the equivalent sub-division in the corresponding provision of these Regulations. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c4c68694d4a1870440cdd005360b6137]

EXPLANATORY NOTE

(This note is not part of the Regulations)

These Regulations consolidate the Immigration (European Economic Area) Regulations 2006 ([S.I. 2006/1003](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/uksi/2006/1003), as amended, “the 2006 Regulations”) implementing [Directive 2004/38/EC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/eudr/2004/0038) of the European Parliament and of the Council of 29th April 2004 on the right of citizens of the Union and their family members to move and reside freely within the territory of the member States (OJ No. L 158, 30.4.04, p77) (“[Directive 2004/38/EC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/eudr/2004/0038)”).

These Regulations give effect to certain judgments of the Court of Justice of the European Union (“CJEU”) and address issues concerning the practical application of [Directive 2004/38/EC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/eudr/2004/0038) within the United Kingdom. As was the case with the 2006 Regulations, these Regulations also apply to nationals from Norway, Iceland, Liechtenstein and Switzerland and their family members as well as to Union citizens and their family members.

These Regulations are similar in form and approach to the 2006 Regulations. Schedule 7 contains a table of equivalences outlining the way in which provisions of the 2006 Regulations correspond to the provisions of these Regulations.

Part 1 (preliminary: regulations 1 to 10) defines the scope of these Regulations and the terms used throughout. A new definition of civil partnerships, marriages and durable partnerships of convenience features in regulation 2(1) (general interpretation). These terms were not defined in the 2006 Regulations.

Regulation 9 contains an exception to the principle that these Regulations do not apply to British citizens. Where a British citizen has exercised any rights under [Directive 2004/38/EC](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/eudr/2004/0038) to reside in another EEA State with a family member who is not also a citizen of an EEA State, in certain circumstances these Regulations will confer a right to reside on those family members upon their return to the United Kingdom with the British citizen. The new regulation 9 contains a requirement for such residence in another EEA State to be “genuine” in order for these Regulations to apply to a British citizen and any family members upon return to the United Kingdom. Regulation 9(3) contains indicative criteria as to the factors to be considered by the Secretary of State when determining whether residence in another EEA State was “genuine”. Regulation 9(4) provides that the regulation is of no application in circumstances where the purpose of the residence of the British citizen in the other EEA State was as a route to circumvent any applicable requirement for any non-EEA family members to have leave to enter or remain in the United Kingdom under the Immigration Act 1971. This gives further effect to the CJEU’s judgment in Case C-456/12 *O & B* (ECLI:EU:C:2014:135). Regulation 9(7) applies adjustments to the conditions for being a qualified person where, for the purposes of regulation 9(1), it is a British citizen who needs to satisfy those conditions.

Part 2 (EEA rights: regulations 11 to 16) sets out the rights to admission and to reside conferred on EEA nationals, their family members and those with derivative rights to reside,

Part 3 (residence documentation: regulations 17 to 22) provides for the issue of residence documentation to those who satisfy the conditions in Part 2. A new regulation 21 permits the Secretary of State to require applications for residence documentation under these Regulations to be made using a specified application form, or pursuant to a particular process. Regulation 21(3) requires an applicant for a residence card or derivative residence card to make the application from within the United Kingdom.

Part 4 (refusal of admission and removal etc.: regulations 23 to 28) provides for the exclusion and removal of EEA nationals and their family members. It is based on the approach adopted by Part 4 of the 2006 Regulations with the addition of a requirement in regulation 27(8) for a court or tribunal to have regard to a new Schedule 1 when they consider whether the requirements of regulation 27 are met. Paragraph 1 of Schedule 1 reflects the margin of appreciation enjoyed by member States to determine their own requirements of public policy and public security, tailored to their own purposes, from time to time (Case 41/74 *Van Duyn* ECLI:EU:C:1974:133). Paragraph 2 onwards of Schedule 1 defines, in terms specific to the United Kingdom, considerations applying to a court or tribunal’s consideration of whether there are grounds of public policy or public security in an individual case, or whether one of the fundamental interests of society (as defined in paragraph 8 of Schedule 1) are affected.

Part 5 (procedure in relation to EEA decisions: regulations 29 to 34) makes provision for the admission and control of those seeking to enter or reside in the United Kingdom under these Regulations.

Parts 6 (appeals under these regulations: regulations 35 to 42) and 7 (general: regulations 43 to 46) are based upon the corresponding Parts of the 2006 Regulations and concern appeals under these Regulations and general matters respectively. Schedule 2 deals with the effect of appeals made under Part 6.

These Regulations come into force on 1st February 2017, subject to transitional provisions, except for regulation 44 and Schedule 5, which come into force on 25th November 2016. Schedule 5 replaces regulation 9 of the 2006 Regulations with a new regulation that mirrors regulation 9 of these Regulations. This change to the 2006 Regulation will apply to all decisions on applications made under those Regulations on or after 25th November 2016, irrespective of whether the application was made prior to Schedule 5 coming into force. This transitory provision aims to bring in the new approach to family members of British citizens as soon as possible.

Part 1 of Schedule 4 revokes the 2006 Regulations subject to the savings and modifications in Part 2 of that Schedule.

The remainder of these Regulations (Schedules 3 and 6) deals with matters consequential to the above changes.

These Regulations extend to the whole of the United Kingdom.

An impact assessment has not been produced for these Regulations as no impact on businesses, charities, voluntary bodies or the public sector is foreseen.
